eṉṟum,
puṟṟilvā ḷaravum añcēṉ
	poyyartam meyyum añcēṉ
kaṟṟivār caṭiemaṇṇal
	kaṇṇutal pātam naṇṇi
maṟṟumōr teyvan taṉṉi
	uṇṭeṉa niṉintuem pemmāṟ
kaṟṟilā tavarik kaṇṭāl
	ammanām añcu māṟē	(accappattu, 1)
eṉṟun tiruvācakattil aruḷicceytiruttal kāṇka.
ivvāṟu piṟappu iṟappu illāc civaperumāṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷātal terittu, ēṉic ciṟuteyvaṅ kaḷellām piṟappu iṟappukaḷuṭ kiṭantu avaṉ āṇi vaḻi yuḻaluñ ciṟṟuyirkaḷātaluṅ kāṭṭic civa camayāciriyar kaḷellārum orē mukamāy ac ciṟuteyva vaṇakkatti maṟuttu vilakkiya centamiḻ aruḷurikaḷ veḷḷiṭi malipōl viḷaṅkik kiṭappavum, iññāṉṟu tammic civareṉavuṅ kūṟik koḷvār cilar, āriyaratu ,cūḻcciyil vīḻntu āriya vētaṅkaḷ civapirāṉ aruḷic ceytaṉavēyā meṉṉum oru poruntāk koḷki yiṉi nāṭṭutaṟkup pukuntu, iṟivaṉṟaṉ tiruvuruva vaḻipāṭṭiṉi ikaḻntu maṟuttut tam vāḻnāḷ elliyaḷavuñ civacamayattukku māṟāy niṉṟa `tayānanta caravati eṉṉum vaṭanāṭṭu muṉivarariṉ koḷkiyit taḻīi irukkuvētattiṟ collappaṭṭa intiraṉ mittiraṉ varuṇaṉ mutalāṉa pala teyvap peyarkaḷellām oru teyvattiṉ mēlaṉavē yāmeṉṟum, āriya vētaṅkaḷiṉ paṭi vēṭkappaṭṭa vēḷvikaḷil uyirkkoli nikaḻavilliyeṉṟum, uyirkkoli ceykaveṉa ēvuñ coṟṟoṭarkaṭ kellām vēṟu poruḷkaḷ uḷaveṉṟun tattamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟellām pēci, ilakkaṇa ilakkiya aḷavi nūl varampukaḷik kaṭantun, toṉṟutoṭṭu varum āriyar tam oḻukalāṟṟukkum vētaṅkaḷ pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ taruma cūttiraṅkaḷ mutalāṉa āriya nūlkaḷil mika virivākavum viḷakkamākavuñ collappaṭṭirukkum uyirkkoli vēḷvi muṟi kaṭkum muṟṟum māṟāṉatoru kōṭpāṭṭiṉik kiḷappiyum periyatōr āravāram purivārāyiṉar.
intiraṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiyē kuṟippatu uṇmiyāyiṉ, appeyarkaḷāl uṇarttappaṭum at teyvaṅkaḷ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭkuriya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ uṭiyavāy iruttal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? intiraṉ cōmaṉ eṉpār pirajāpatiyāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭavar eṉac `catapatapirāmaṇaṅ (11, 1, 6) kūṟutalāṉum,mittiraṉ, varuṇaṉ, tātri, aryamāṉ, amcaṉ, pakaṉ, vivavataṉ, ātityaṉ eṉṉum eṇmarum atitiyiṉ putalvareṉa aḵtu aṅṅaṉamē eṭuttuc collutalāṉum, kaṭkuṭiyum vilaṅkukaḷiṉ koliyāl varum ūṉuṇavum av intiraṉ mutaliyōr kikkoṇṭamiyun tantiyik kollal makaḷip puṇartal mutalāṉa mika iḻinta ceyalkaḷi avar purintamiyum mēlē 372 ām pakkam mutal 380 ām pakkam variyil emmāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭiruttalāṉum, ittēvarkaḷ pakivarāṟ palakālun tōlviyuṟṟu varunti vantamiyiṉ tām cāvātiruttaṟku maruntu vēṇṭi muyaṉṟa kālattil avarik kollum nañcu oṉṟu eḻa ataṉic civapirāṉ uṭkoṇṭu at tēvarkaḷik kāttami kēsi viṣaya pātrēṇa yat rutrēṇā pipat cahā (10, 136, 7) eṉa irukku vētattiṉ kaṇ naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭirut talāṉum at tēvarellārum makkaḷōṭotta ciṟṟuyir kaḷēyāva rallatu muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷarātal cellātu. maṟṟuc, civapirāṉō uyirkaṭkuḷḷa ik kuṟṟaṅkaḷ ciṟitum aṇukap peṟātavaṉākaliṉ avaṉ oruvaṉē ellā mutaṉmiyum uṭiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷātalum mēlē viḷakkappaṭṭatu. iṉṉum itaṉ virivi vēḷāḷar nākarikam eṉṉum ematu nūliṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
āriyar uyirkaḷi ērāḷamāyk koṉṟu tām vaṇaṅki vanta ciṟuteyvaṅkaḷukku avaṟṟiṉ iṟicciyip paṭittut tāmum ayiṉṟuvantami irukkuvētat tilēyē paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷilun teḷivākac collap paṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. ataṉ mutaṉ maṇṭilattu, 116 ām patikattiṉ 7 āvatu ceyyuḷil acuviṉi tēvarkaḷ cukṣīvāṉukku nūṟu cāṭic cārāyaṅ koṭuttamiyum, 16 āvatu ceyyuḷil `rijirācuvaṉ acuviṉi tēvarkaḷiṉ ūrtiyāṉa kaḻutiyiṉporuṭṭu vitiyaṭitta nūṟu cemmaṟiyāṭukaḷi veṭṭi avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi uṇavākap paṭittamiyuñ collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; 162,  163 ām patikaṅkaḷil veḷḷāṭṭiṉiyuṅ kutiriyiyuṅ koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉic camittup `pūṣaṉ `intiraṉ eṉṉun teyvaṅkaṭkup paṭittami virivākak kūṟappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu; ataṉ aintām maṇṭilattu 29 ām patikattiṉ 7 āvatu ceyyuḷil munnūṟu erumi māṭukaḷik koṉṟuavaṟṟiṉ iṟicciyi neruppil vāṭṭip patappaṭutti ataṉi mūṉṟu periya toṭṭic cōmac cārāyattuṭaṉ intiraṉukkup paliyūṭṭiṉami veḷippaṭi yāka nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu; ataṉ āṟām maṇṭilattu 17 ām patikattiṉ 11 āvatu ceyyuḷilum aṅṅaṉamē nūṟu erumi māṭukaḷik koṉṟu tīyiṟ patappaṭutti avvūṉi mūṉṟu periya toṭṭik kaḷḷuṭaṉ intiraṉukkup paṭittami teḷivāka moḻiyappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu; 4 āvatu ceyyuḷil āriyar tammuḷ iṟantupōṉa oruvaṉic cuṭukāṭṭiṉ viṟakiṉmēl vittuk koḷuttap pōkiyil ōr āṭṭik koṉṟu ataṉ uṟuppukaḷ ovvoṉṟiyum piṇamāyk kiṭappavaṉ uṟuppukaḷiṉ mēl vittu avaṉik koḷut tiṉami kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu;am maṇṭilattu 85ām patikattiṉ 13 āvatu ceyyuḷil oruvaṉukkum oruttikkum maṇam naṭattuvataṟkumuṉ makamīṉ nāḷiṟ kāḷimāṭukaḷik koṉṟu pakalavaṉukkup paliyūṭṭi, ataṉpiṟ paṅkuṉi nāḷil maṇam naṭattumuṟi pakarappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu; am maṇṭilattu 86 ām patikattiṉ 14 āvatu ceyyuḷil āriyar patiṉintu erumi māṭukaḷik koṉṟu intiraṉukkup paṭikka, avaṉ avaṟṟiṉ koḻuppi ārattiṉṟu vayiṟu niṟintaṭinta makiḻcci eṭutturikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu; am maṇṭilattu 91 ām patikattiṉ 14 āvatu ceyyuḷiṟ kutirikaḷiyum erutu kaḷiyum ākkaḷiyum vaṟaṭṭu ākkaḷiyum cemmaṟi yāṭukaḷiyum koṉṟu āriyar vēḷvi vēṭṭami viḷakkamāka viḷampappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. irukku vētattiṟkup piṉ eḻunta `pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ `mirutikaḷ eṉpavaṟṟil virivākac collappaṭṭirukkum  uyirkkoli vēḷvikaḷ tām irukkuvēta kālattiṟkuppiṉ uṇṭā yiṉaveṉṟu kūṟattuṇipavar, irukkuvētattilēyē orukālaṉṟip palakālum iṅṅaṉam viḷakkamāka nuvalappaṭṭirukkuṅ kuṭi koli malinta veṟiyāṭṭu vēḷvikaḷ nikaḻavilli yeṉṟalum, avi tamakku vēṟuporuḷkaḷ uḷaveṉṟalum `muḻup pūcaṉikkāyic ciṟu cōṟṟil maṟippataṟkē  oppāy ārāycci yaṟivuṭiyārāl nakiyāṭi viṭukkaṟpālaṉavāy oḻiyum eṉka. ivi tamakkellām vēṟu poruḷkaḷ uḷaveṉpār kūṟṟut, `tavavoḻukka meṉpatu ētōr uyiriyuṅ koṉṟu ataṉ ūṉi uṇṇāmiyēyām eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutti auvip pirāṭṭiyār moḻinta nōṉ peṉpatu koṉṟu tiṉṉāmi eṉṉum aṟivurit toṭarit taṉ karuttukku iṇaṅkumāṟu poruḷpaṭutta vēṇṭiṉāṉ ōr ūṉtiṉṉi `tiṉṉāmi eṉṉum orucollit `tiṉ āmi eṉa iraṇṭu collākavun, `tiṉ ā mi eṉa mūṉṟu collākavum pirittu `āmi iṟicciyit tiṉṉal eṉavum, `māṭu ākiya eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iṟicciyit tiṉṉal eṉavum poru ḷurittu, `ākavē `tavavoḻukka meṉpatu āmi māṭu āṭu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ ūṉi yuṇavākak koḷḷutalēyām eṉṟu av vaṟivurikkup poruḷ kūṟi āravāram puritalōṭu oppatāmaṉṟip piṟiteṉṉi? oru nūliṉ pōkkiyum ataṉi iyaṟṟiya āciriyar karuttiyum piṟaḻttit, tām vēṇṭiyavāṟellām uri yurippatum meyyuri yākumā?
eṉavē, teṉṉāṭṭuc civamuṉivarar evaratu aruḷuriyun tamatu maruḷurikku iṭantantu utavi ceyyāmiyiṉ, at teyva muṉivararit tamakku mēṟkōḷākak koḷḷutal ākāmi kaṇṭa in nāṭṭup pōlic civar cilarum māyāvāta vētāntikaḷ palarun tamatu pōlikkoḷkikku icinta vaṭanāṭṭu muṉivarar tayānanta caravatiyi aṭikkalam pukkatu cālap poruttamēyām. pukkum eṉ! avar kikkoṇṭa irukku vētamē avaratu kōṭpāṭṭiṉ poymmi kāṭṭi avarik kiviṭṭatāyiṉ, tayānanta caravati avark keṅṅaṉam utavi ceyyavallār!
aṟṟēl, aḵtāka, āriyamoḻi nūlkaḷākiya `irukku, `ecur mutalivaṟṟil tamiḻc cāṉṟōrtaṅ kōṭpāṭukaḷ pukuntavāṟu eṉṉiyeṉiṟ kūṟutum: āriyar nalluṇavum nalluṟiviṭamum peṟutaṟkut tēṭiyalintu tamiḻ nilamākiya ip parata nāṭṭiṟpukunta kālattup perumpālum pōr mēṟkoṇṭē pōntaṉar; appōtu tamiḻarkaḷ nākarikattiṉ mikkārāy, āṅkāṅku nakaraṅkaḷum, an nakaraṅkaḷil araciyalkaḷum amittup poruḷvaliyum paṭivaliyum pōr valiyum mikap paṭittavarkaḷāy vāḻntu vantamiyiṉ, pacittuvanta vaṟiyarākiya āriyar paṇṭit tamiḻmakkaḷi etirttu vellutal iyalātāyiṟṟu. iyalātākavē palavakiyālun tamiḻar tam naṭpiyum uṟaviyum tēvanāṭṭiliruntu vanta tēvarkaḷēyeṉṟum, tām pēcummoḻi tēvamoḻiyē yeṉṟun, tammip pakittalun tamakku iṭar ceytalum aṅṅaṉañ ceyvāri mīḷānarakiṟku āḷākku meṉṟun, tammi vaṇaṅkit tām ēviya ceytoḻukuvārkku immiyil ellā nalaṅkaḷum perukuvataṉṟi maṟumiyilum pitirarkaḷāl uvantēṟṟu uyarnta iṉpa ulakaṅkaḷil vikkappaṭuvatuṅ kikūṭu meṉṟuñ collit tamiḻariṟ perumpālāri āriyar tam vayappaṭuttiviṭṭār. aññāṉṟi āriyariṟ perumpālār vaṭakkē paṉimikunta nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantavarkaḷātaliṉ aḻakiya veṇṇiṟamuḷḷa uṭampum nīla viḻikaḷum vāyntavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷatu aḻakiya tōṟṟattik kaṇṭa aññāṉṟit tamiḻariṟ palar, avvāriyar colli meyyeṉa nampi avarkaḷit tammiṉum uyarnta tēvarkaḷākavē eṇṇik koṇṭāṭi avarkkup paṇintoḻukalāyiṉar. ivvaḷavukkut tamiḻarit taṅ kīḻppaṭutta iṭampeṟṟa āriyarkku iṉi iṅku ākātatu eṉ uḷatu! āriyar kaṭavuḷarākavum, avar koṇarnta āriyamoḻi kaṭavuḷar moḻiyākavum, ammoḻiyil avar pāṭiya pāṭṭukku `irukku `ecur `cāmam eṉṉum vaṭiviṟ puruṣamētattil (āṇ makaṉikkoṉṟu vēṭṭa vēḷviyil) tōṉṟiyaṉavākavum (ivvāṟu irukkuvēta puruṭacūtta mantirattil eḻutivikkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu). piṉṉuñ cilakālaṅ kaḻintapiṉ ivvētaṅkaḷ civapirāṉālēyē aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭaṉavākavum vittut tamiḻar koṇṭāṭum paṭi ceytuviṭṭaṉar.
ivvaḷavil amiyātu, āriya moḻi yallāta tamiḻ mutaliyaṉa makkaḷāl ākkappaṭṭaṉa vākaliṉ avaṟṟiṉkaṇ variyappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ āriyarāl ēṟkaṟpālaṉa allaveṉavum, āriyamoḻiyil uḷḷa nūlkaḷē talimēṟkoṇṭu ēṟṟaṟkuriyaṉa veṉavum, āriya iṉattavarallāta tamiḻarum piṟarum āriyamoḻi nūlkaḷi ōtutaṟ kurimiyuṭiyaralla reṉavum, tamiḻarumpiṟarum āriyar ēviya paṇiceytu oḻukutal oṉṟaṟkē uriyareṉavum moḻintu nāṭcellac cellat tamatu mutaṉmiyiṉi nāṭṭutaṟku vēṇṭum ēṟpāṭukaḷi yellām āriyar orē kaṭṭuppāṭāyiruntu tiṟamāyc ceytukoṇṭaṉar. tamiḻaril valiyarāyuḷḷa aracar kaḷiyuñ celvavāḻkkiyiṟ ciṟanta vaṇikarkaḷiyun tamatu mutaṉmi nilipeṟāteṉak kaṇṭu, aracariyum vaṇikariyum maṭṭun tamakkuk kīḻ orupaṭi iraṇṭupaṭi maṭṭiliṟakki, avarkaḷukku, `kṣattiriyar, `viciyar eṉṉum peyarkaḷic ciṟappākat taruvatupōṟ ṟantu, avarkaḷ makaḷirōṭu tām kalakkalām, āṉāl avarkaḷ tam makaḷiroṭu kalakkalākāteṉṟum, aṅṅaṉaṅ kalapparāyiṉ avar peruntīviṉikkuāḷākuvareṉṟum tamakkuk kīḻ iv viruvakuppiṉar maṭṭun tammuṭiya āriyanūlkaḷi ōtutaṟku urimiyuṭiyareṉṟuñ colli avviruvakuppāriyun tam miṉattavarākki, avvāṟṟāl tamatu mutaṉmi avar tamakkuḷḷum nilipeṟutaṟku iṭañceytukoṇṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉamāka āriyaratu cūḻcciyāṟ paḻantamiḻ makkaḷukkuṭ periyatoru māṟutal nikaḻntu paravavē āriyarār uyarntavarākap pārāṭṭap paṭutalum, āriyamoḻiyik kaṟpatum, āriyanūlvaḻi yoḻukutalum, tamiḻiyun tamiḻvaḻaṅkum ēḻimakkaḷiyum iḻinta nilimikkaṭ paṭuttup pēcutalun tamakkuc ciṟapput taruvaṉavākat tamiḻarilēyē kaṟṟavaruñ celvarumāyuḷḷār karutuvārāyiṉar. ataṉāl, tamiḻaril araca vakuppiṉarum piṟarum āriyamoḻiyi virumpik kaṟṟu, ataṉkaṇ uriyāṭutalum nūl eḻututaluñ ceyvārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉan tamiḻarkkuḷ āriyamoḻiyum āriyar tam vaḻakkavoḻukkaṅ kaḷum paravavē, avvāriya vaḻakkukaḷukku oruvāṟṟāl uṭaṉpaṭ ṭiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟumiyum tamiḻ uṭaṉpaṭ ṭiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṉciṟumiyum tamiḻ vaḻakkukaḷiṉ perumiyum puṭipaṭa oṟṟi ārāyntaḷan tuṇarntu uṇmi kaṇṭa `jaṉakaṉ `ajātacatturu, `avapatiki kēyaṉ `piravākaṉajivali mutalāṉa tamiḻvēntarkaḷ, amayam nērntuḻiyellām āriya muṉivarkkut tamiḻccāṉṟōr nuṇuki yaṟinta meypporuḷkaḷi avarkkuriya āriyamoḻiyilēyē yeṭuttut teruṭṭi, avarkku meyyaṟivu koḷutti, avar tam āriyak  kōṭpāṭukaḷ payaṉilavātaṉ mēlun tīviṉip palavātaluṅ kāṭṭi varalāyiṉar. ivvāṟu tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ āriyarkku aṟivuṟutti vanta meyyurit tiraṭṭukaḷē `upaniṭataṅkaḷ eṉap peyarpeṟuvavāyiṉa. it tamiḻvēntarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷā yiruttal koṇṭu avarellām āriyar pōlumeṉa mayaṅki viṭaṟka. cēkkiḻār aruḷiya tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattiluḷḷa civaṉaṭiyār kaḷiṉ taṉittamiḻ iyaṟ peyarkaḷ palavaṟṟi vaṭanūlār tamatu vaṭamoḻiyil moḻapeyarttu vittuk koṇṭavāṟu pōla, av vēntarkaḷiṉ paṇṭit taṉittamiḻp peyarkaḷum avvāṟu āriyamoḻiyil moḻipeyarttu amikkap paṭṭaṉa veṉṟu uṇarntu koḷka. iṉṉum tamiḻaraca vakuppiṉiṉṟun tuṟavupukunta `vicuvāmittirar, `tēvāpi mutalāṉa muṉivararkaḷum āriya moḻiyik kaṟṟu atiṟ perumpulamimikku, āriyar kikkoṇṭu pōtaruñ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkattiyum uyirkkoli veṟiyāṭṭu vēḷvikaḷiyun tolippāṉ vēṇṭiyē ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ tī eṉṉum oḷivaṭivukaṇ mēlum, avaṟṟiṉkaṇ ṇellām aruḷoḷi vaṭiviṉarāy viḷaṅkuñ civapirāṉ mēlum paṟpala patikaṅkaḷi āriyarkku māṟāy avartam āriya moḻiyilēyē iyaṟṟi, avi tammit tām muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭuṅ kālaṅkaḷiṟ payaṉ paṭuttivantaṉar. ivvāṟu vicuvāmittirar mutalāyiṉār āriyamoḻiyiṟ patikaṅkaḷum nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉum avarellān tamiḻ vakuppiṉarātali yuṇarntaṉṟē manumirutiyum (10, 45),
irupiṟappāḷa rallāta maṟṟi ellā vakuppiṉarum milēccartam moḻiyip pēciṉum allatu āriyartam moḻiyip pēciṉum avarellān tayukkaḷēyāvar
eṉak kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl āriyarallāta tamiḻarum piṟarum akkālattil āriyamoḻiyip pēciṉāreṉpatu peṟappaṭu kiṉṟataṉṟō?
tayukka ḷeṉappaṭṭa tamiḻaril, aracarāyiṉāriyum avaruṭ ṭuṟavunili pukkāriyuñ celvavaḷa muṭi yāriyum vēṟu pōkkiṉmiyālē kṣattiriya viciya vakuppiṉkaṭ cērttukkoṇṭa āriyar aṅṅaṉañ cērkkappaṭṭa avarkaḷ tamakku māṟāṉa tayukkaḷē eṉpataṉi amayam vāyttuḻiyellām veḷippaṭi yākac colliyē vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. `ajīkarttaṉ eṉṉum āriyap pārppaṉaṉ, taṉmakaṉ `cunasēpaṉi vēḷvikkaḷattil veṭṭi varuṇaṉukkup paliyūṭṭa vēṇṭik kēṭṭa `rōkitaṉ eṉpavaṉpāl nūṟu kaṟavimāṭukaḷi vāṅkikkoṇṭu avaṉi avaṉukku viṟṟuviṭṭatu maṉṟi, vēḷvikkaḷattiṟ koṇarappaṭṭa taṉmakaṉik kaḻuttaṟuppataṟku vēṟu yārun tuṇiyārāyp piṉ vāṅkiniṟṟal kaṇṭu, taṉakku iṉṉum irunūṟu kaṟavikaḷ koṭuttāl tāṉē taṉ makaṉ kaḻutti veṭṭuvatākac colli, aṅṅaṉamē irunūṟu kaṟavikaḷ koṭukkap peṟṟukkoṇṭu avviḷiñaṉ kaḻuttik kattikoṇṭu ariyappōkiyil, ammakaṉ taṉtantiyiṉ koṭuñceyalikkaṇṭu alaṟik kaṭavuḷari vēṇṭi aḻāniṟpa, atukaṇṭu peritum neñcaṅkarinta vicuvāmittirar ac cunasēpaṉi akkolikkut tappuvittu, avaṉit tam makkaḷ ellārkkum mūtta talimakaṉāka ēṟṟuc ciṟappucceytamiyum, atē nērattil ac cunasēpaṉ vicuvāmittirari nōkki, ō paratar kaḷukkut talivarē eṉṟu viḷittu nuṅkaḷ putalvarkaḷ icintāl, eṉakku naṉmiyuṇṭāmāṟum yāṉ taṅkaṭku makaṉān taṉmiyaṭiyumāṟum avarkaḷ eṉpāl nēyamāy irukkak kaṟpiyuṅkaḷeṉṟu vēṇṭa, avarum ataṟku oruppaṭṭuk tammakkaḷ nūṟupērkkun tam tuṇipu terivikka, avaruḷ `matuccantar eṉappaṭum iḷiñar aimpatiṉmarkku mūtta aimpatiṉmar maṭṭum ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭārāka, ataṉāl vicuvāmittirar avarmēṟ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu num vaḻiyil varuvār in nāṭṭi viṭṭakaṉṟu itaṉ ellip puṟaṅkaḷil irukka eṉṟu vitamiyāl am marapiṉarē āntirar, puṇṭarar, caparar, puḷintar, mūtipar āṉamiyum, tayukkaḷiṟ perumpālār vicuvāmittirar vaḻittōṉṟiṉamiyum aitarēya pirāmaṇam ēḻām iyalilil (13 mutal 18 variyil teḷitturikkap paṭṭiruttal kāṇka. itaṉāl, āriyappārppaṉar tām poruṇmēl vitta pēravāvāl ettakiya koṭun toḻiluñ ceytaṟkup piṉ iṭi yārātalum, tamiḻc cāṉṟōr iṭaruṟṟa āriyar māṭṭum pērirakkamuṭiyarāy avarkkup pērutavi purivārātalum, āriyarallāta tayukka ḷellārkkum piṟappiṭamāvār vicuvāmittira marapiṉarē yātalum, tayukkaḷiṟ ciṟanta `paratarkku vicuvāmittirarē mutaṟkuravarātalum, āriyar nērattiṟkut takkavāṟu tammavarallātāri orukāl tayukkaḷeṉa ikaḻntum piṟitorukāl kṣattiriyar viciyareṉat tammōṭu iṉappaṭuttip pukaḻntun tamiḻari ēmāṟṟum nīrarātalum iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉavallavō? iṉṉum, vicuvāmittira marapiṉar uyarnta meypporu ḷuṇarcci yuṭiyareṉ patūum, tām aritiṉ uṇarnta meypporuḷkaḷi, avi yuṇarāta āriyamarapiṉ vaḻivanta cunasēpaṉukku avar aṟivuṟuttiṉa reṉpatūum, vicuvāmittira marapiṉarāṉa kātiṉarkku āriyavētaṅkaḷiṉ vēṟākat teyvattaṉmi poruntiya maṟṟoru vētam iruntateṉpatūum26 av aitarēya pirāmaṇamē naṉku uri tarukiṉṟatu. iv vicuvāmittira marapiṉarkkuriya teyvikavētamē aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉṉum nāṟporu ḷiyalpukaḷiyum, civapirāṉiyum, avaṉ muṉittu viḷakkum muttī vaḻipāṭukaḷiyum uṇarttum paṇṭit tamiḻvēta mātalun tāṉē peṟappaṭum.
iṉṉun, tamakku utaviyun tuṇiyumā yiruntu, tāñceytu pōnta uyirkkoli vēḷvikaḷi naṭatti vanta variyil tamiḻari kṣattiriyarāka vittut tammōṭiṉappaṭuttik koṇṭāṭiya āriyar, piṉṉart tammiavar oru poruṭ paṭuttārāṉavaḻi, uṭaṉē avari iḻintavarākkiviṭa lāyiṉar. itu, vēḷvi yāṟṟāmi yālum pirāmaṇarōṭu uṟavu kalavāmiyālum, kṣattiriya marapiṉarāṉa puṇṭarakar, ōṭrar, tirāviṭar, kāmpōjar, yavaṉar, cakar, pāratar, pallavar, cīṉar, kirātar, taratar, kacar, eṉpār varavarac cātippuṟampāṉa viruṣalariṉ niliyi yaṭintaṉar eṉa manuvum (10, 13, 44), māpārata anucācaṉa paruvamum (2103) urikkumāṟṟāl naṉkuṇarappaṭum. mēṟkuṟitta tamiḻaraca marapiṉar aṉivariyum āriyar `tayukkaḷ eṉavē koṇṭu ikaḻntaṉa reṉpataṟku, māpārata turōṇa paruvam (4747), āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kāmpōjar, cakar, caparar, kirātar, varvarar eṉpāriṉ kurutiyiṉālun taciyiṉālum iv vaḻakiya nilattic `cinēyaṉ cōṟṟut tiraḷi yākkiṉāṉ; kattarikkappaṭṭut taliyil mayir ilarāyiṉum nīṇṭa tāṭimayiriṉi yuṭiya tayukkaḷiṉ talikaḷālum; avar tam muṭikaḷālum mūṭuṇṭa nilam, iṟakiḻanta paṟavikaḷāl mūṭuṇṭa nilampōṉ ṟiruntatu eṉak kūṟutalē cāṉṟām. itaṉō ṭoppavē arivamcamum (773), cakar,yavaṉar, kāmpōjar, pāratar, pallavar, kōlicarppar, makiṣar, tārvar, cōḻar, kēraḷar mutaliya aṉivarum kṣattiriya rākavēyiruntaṉar; āṉāl vaciṭṭaruṭiya collāl tūṇṭappaṭṭa ciṟanta cakaravēntaṉāl avarkaḷ tamakkuriya vāḻkki yuyarviṉiyuñ camaya niliyiṉiyum iḻakkalāyiṉareṉa urittal kāṇka. mēlē kuṟittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa iṉattavar paṟpalarum paṇṭit tamiḻ vakuppiṉarātalum, āriyar iṅku varutaṟku muṉṉarē in nāṭṭiṉ paṟpala pakutikaḷiluṅ kuṭiyēṟi avaṟṟi aracāṇṭa attamiḻ makkaḷ tampāl vantu aṭikkalam pukunta āriyar tam cūḻalkaḷiṟ cikkuṇṭu avar tañ coṟpaṭi, naṭantu vanta variyil avar tammi `kṣattiriyar `viciyar eṉap peyar vittuc cīrāṭṭivanta āriyar, piṉṉart tam tīviṉic ceyalkaḷik kaṇṭu tammi at tamiḻmakkaḷ aruvaruttu viṭṭa vuṭaṉē avar tamakkellām `tayukkaḷ eṉa ōr iḻinta peyar vittu avari oruṅkē iḻitturikkalāṉatum, ivvāṟākat tamiḻari iḻitturikkumāṟu āriyari ēviṉavar āriyarkkuk kuruvāṉa vaciṭṭarēyātalum mēṟkāṭṭiya vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ urikaḷiṉālēyē naṉku teḷiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa allavō?
iṉi,  iññāṉṟu tammip pārppaṉareṉa uyarttup pēcikkoḷvārum, ap pārppaṉarip piṉpaṟṟi yoḻuki avvāṟṟāl tamakku uyarvu tēṭikkoḷvārum ēṉip potumakkaḷi iḻinta nilimikkāṭpaṭuttu naṭattum iyalpukaḷi uṟṟukkāṇa vallārkkup, paṇṭik kālattup pārppaṉariṉ oḻukalāṟukaḷum ivar tammōṭotta iyalpiṉavām uṇmi teṟṟeṉap pulaṉākāniṟkum. mēlnāṭṭiliruntu pōntu innāṭṭi aracāḷum āṅkila naṉmakkaḷiṉ moḻiyiyum avartam naṭiyuṭi vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum piṉpaṟṟi avarpāṟ pala ciṟappukkaḷiyum peṟa muntuvōr pārppaṉarēyāvar. ēṉip potumakkaḷiṉ vēṟākat tammip pirittu uyarttutaṟkum, celva vāḻkki yiṟ ṟalimipeṟṟu vāḻtaṟkum ētuvāvatu āṅkila makkaḷiṉ nākarikattit taḻuvi yoḻukutalē eṉak kaṇṭukoṇṭa nāṉ toṭṭup, pārppaṉar perumpālum āṅkila moḻiyiyē kaṟṟalum, ataṉālēyē uriyāṭu talum, ēṉit tamiḻariyun tamiḻiyuñ `cūttirar, `cūttira pāṣi eṉa ikaḻtaluñ ceytal evarum uṇarntatēyām. iṅṅaṉamē paṇṭināḷi lirunta pārppaṉaruñ, tammit tamiḻariṉ vēṟāka uyarttutaṟku vāyilāvatu āriyamoḻiyiyum āriya roḻukalāṟṟiyum piṉpaṟṟutalē eṉak kaṇṭavaḷavāṉē āriya moḻiyik kaṟṟu āriya vaḻakkukaḷit taḻuvit, tamiḻarellāriyun `tayukkaḷ eṉavun tamiḻariṉ moḻiyi `milēccamoḻi eṉavum ikaḻnturippā rāyiṉar. iññāṉṟip pārppaṉar, tamiḻariṟ ciṟanta aracariyum celva vāḻkkiyiṟ periya vaṇikariyum avvāṟikaḻn totukkutal ākāmal avarit tām cārntu piḻikkavēṇṭiyiruttal kaṇṭu, avari `kṣattiriya viciya vakuppiṉpāṟ paṭuttuk koṇṭāṭutal pōlavē, paṇṭip pārppaṉarun tamiḻ aracariyum vaṇikariyum `kṣattiriya viciya vakuppiṉpāṟpaṭuttuk koṇṭāṭalāyiṉar. tiruvaṉanti aracar, micūr aracar mutalāyiṉāri `kṣattiriyar eṉavum, nāṭṭukkōṭṭi vaṇikari `viciyar eṉavuṅ koṇṭāṭi, avarpāluḷḷa poruṭṭiraḷiyum piṟanalaṅkaḷiyum iññāṉṟip pārppaṉar koḷḷi koṇṭu nukartal kāṇka. ivar tam pārāṭṭuriyil mayaṅki iv varacarum iv vaṇikarun tammi ap pārppaṉarukku aṭutta niliyil uyarntavarākak karuti vittukkoṇṭu tam miṉattavarāṉa ēṉit tamiḻmakkaḷellāriyuñ `cūttirar eṉa ikaḻnturittu oḻukutal pōlavē, paṇṭit tamiḻmaṉṉarum vaṇikarum pārppaṉartam poyp pārāṭṭuriyil vīḻntu tammiṉattavariyun tamiḻiyum puṟampaḻittu vantaṉar. iṉṉum iññāṉṟu, pārppaṉar tam pārāṭṭiṉiyē peritākak karutip piḻippā rellārun, tammōṭotta tamiḻarpāṟ ciṟitum uṟavukalakka iciyārāy, ap pārppaṉarpālum uṟavukalattal iciyātēṉum avar tam puṟakkaṭi vāyiliṟ kāttiruntu avar vīciyeṟiyavum ecciṟ cōṟṟi yuṇṭu, ap pārppaṉartan teyvat taṉmiyiṉiyum avartam āriyamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ teyvat taṉmiyiṉiyum ap pārppaṉariṉum pārkka uyarttup pēcit `tirunāvukkaracar `meykaṇṭa tēvar, `cēkkiḻār mutalāṉa aruṭ pērāciriyarkaḷiyum pārppaṉar kūṟuñ cūttira vakuppiṉpāṟ paṭuttu,avar aruḷicceyta aruḷ nūlkaḷiyum vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu kaṭaṉkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa vāka moḻintu, uṇmikku māṟāṉa poyppaṭukuḻiyil vīḻntu alakipōl alaṟutalpōlavē, aññāṉṟip pārppaṉar tam pārāṭṭiṉi viḻinta tamiḻaruṅ kōṭarikkāmpi yoppat tammiṉattavarkkun tamatu tamiḻmoḻikkum iḻitta tīṅkukaḷ aḷappila. tamiḻarkkun tamiḻkkun tamiḻnāṭṭukkum āriyarāl viḷinta pollāṅkukaḷi viṭat, tammi av āriya iṉattiṟ cērttukkoṇṭa pārppaṉarālum, avar tammāl otukkappaṭṭiruntē avar tam pārāṭṭuriyiṉi mēlatākak karuti avarvaḻic cārntoḻukiya tamiḻarālum viḷinta pollāṅkukaḷē mikka koṭiyaṉavāmeṉṟu uṇarntu koḷka.
iññāṉṟu āriyar eṉa oru taṉi vakuppār kāṇappaṭṭilarēṉun, tamiḻariliruntē peruntokiyiṉar āriyarāy māṟip pārppaṉar peyar puṉintu vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷi ōtit, tamiḻarkkun tamiḻmoḻikkun, tamiḻtteyvak koḷkikkum māṟāy niṉṟu varutal pōlavē, aññāṉṟun tamiḻvakuppiṉarum piṟarurum peruntoki yiṉarāy āriyar oḻukalāṟukaḷik kikkoṇṭu vaṭanūlkaḷip payiṉṟu tamiḻvaḻakkukku muḻumāṟāy niṉṟaṉar eṉka. ip peṟṟiyiṉarāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉavē vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷa pala pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ, kalpa cūttiraṅkaḷ, mīmāñci, poykkati potinta purāṇaṅkaḷ mutalāyiṉa vākumeṉṟu uṇarntukoḷka. iṉi, iññāṉṟum pārppaṉar kaṭṭiya poy vaḻakkukaḷiṉ peṟṟitēṟṟi, aruḷoḻukkamāṭciyum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭun teruṭṭuvār uḷarātal pōlavē, aññāṉṟuṅkuṭi koli malinta āriya vēḷviyiṉ tīviṉip peṟṟiyuñ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkac ciṟumiyun tēṟṟi aṉpum aruḷum perukacceyta tamiḻccāṉṟōrum uḷar. avar aruḷic ceytaṉavē: muttī vēḷviyum uruttira vaḻipāṭuñ ciṟanteṭuttuk kūṟum irukku ecur cāma atarva vētap patikaṅkaḷ cila palavum, uṇmivaḻā upaniṭataṅkaḷum, cāṅkiya pōka vicēṭika niyāyika vētānta cūttiraṅkaḷum, maṟcam vāyu mutalāṉa cila purāṇaṅkaḷum, puṭkaram, mirukēntiram mutalāṉa cila civakāmaṅkaḷum piṟavumām. iññāṉṟum āṅkila moḻiyikkaṟṟu ataṉkaṇ nalaṅkiḷarum nūlkaḷ pala iyaṟṟun tamiḻarkaḷum ēṉi vakuppiṉarum uḷarāyiṉāṟpōlavē, aññāṉṟum āriyamoḻiyik kaṟṟu ataṉkaṭ payaṉ perukum nālvētap patikaṅkaḷum upaniṭatam mutaliya aṟivunūlkaḷum iyaṟṟiṉa tamiḻccaṉṟōr palarum uḷarāṉār eṉṟu teḷika. iññāṉṟi āṅkilamoḻiyil āṅkilarum āṅkila rallāta piṟarum iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷil avvavar iyaṟkikaḷum avvavarkkuriya kōṭpāṭukaḷum pirittaṟi yalāmpaṭi viḷaṅkik kiṭattal pōlavē, aññāṉṟi āriya moḻi yuḷum āriyarum avarip piṉpaṟṟiṉārun tamiḻccāṉṟōrum ēṉip piṟarum iyaṟṟiya patikaṅkaḷum nūlkaḷum avaravarkkuriya iyaṟkiyuṅ kōṭpāṭukaḷum poruntap peṟṟaṉavāy, nuṇṇaṟiviṉārkku, ivi āriyar iyaṟṟiṉa, ivitamiḻar iyaṟṟiṉa ivi ēṉip piṟar iyaṟṟiṉaveṉṟu pirittaṟiyalāmpaṭi viḷaṅki niṟkumeṉka. eṉavē, āriyamoḻiyil tamiḻccāṉṟōr, aruḷic ceyta patikaṅkaḷum nūlkaḷum viravi niṟkum iyalpu ārāycci yaṟivuṭiyārk kellān teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkik kiṭakku meṉka. paṇṭutoṭṭē palvēṟu nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa palvēṟu makkaṭ piriviṉarun tattam nāṭukaḷi viṭṭakaṉṟu ēṉi nāṭukaḷil uḷḷa ēṉi makkaḷōṭu kalantuṟavāṭi vantaṉa rākaliṉ, avar tammuḷ oru vakuppiṉar ēṉi vakuppiṉariṉ naṭiyuṭi vaḻakkaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟi yoḻukutalum avaratu moḻiyit tāṅ kaṟṟalum am moḻiyil tām nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟutalum iyaṟkiyāy nikaḻap peṟumāṟu varalāṟṟu nūluṇarcci yuṭiyārkkellām iṉitu viḷaṅkik kiṭappatoṉṟēyām. ākavē, tamiḻccāṉṟōr aruḷic ceyta patikaṅkaḷum, nūlkaḷum, avartaṅ kōṭpāṭukaḷum āriyamoḻiyiṟ pukuntu nilipeṟalāṉatu oru putumi yaṉṟeṉa ōrntu koḷka.
eṉṟitukāṟum viḷakkiyavāṟṟāṟ, piṟappu iṟappu illā aruḷoḷi vaṭiviṉaṉākiya ellāmvalla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ, ettuṇiyum periya taṉatu muḻumutaṟṟaṉmiyi ettuṇiyuñ ciṟiya emmaṉōr oruvāṟṟāṉāyiṉuṅ kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭu uytaṟporuṭṭu, emmaṉōr kaṇkaṭkup pulaṉām neruppoḷiyil viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiniṟkum uṇmiyiṉi mutaṉmutaṟ kaṇṭaṟintōr paratar eṉṉum paṇṭit taṉit talimit tamiḻccāṉṟōrē yāva reṉpatūum, av vuṇmikku māṟākāmalē irukku vētam aṉal vaḻipāṭṭi ōtum iṭaṅkaḷilellām ataṉik kaṇṭaṟintavar `paratarē āvar eṉavum, ataṉāl aṉaṟ kaṭavuḷum ak kaṭavuḷaṟku uyirāy viḷaṅkuñ civapirāṉum `pārataṉ `parataṉ eṉappeyar peṟuvareṉavun teḷiyak kūṟuvatāyiṟ ṟeṉpatūum, tīppiḻampiṉ vāyilāka ellām valla mutalvaṉi nērē kaṇṭuvaṇaṅkum māpperu vaṇakkatti mutaṉmutaṟ kaṇṭavar teṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭiṉkaṭ paṇṭirunta tamiḻc cāṉṟōrēyātal paṟṟiyē tīkkaṭavuḷ vaṇakkattit teṉṟicik kuriyatākki ataṉit `takṣiṇākkini eṉa vaṭanūlkaḷellām orumukamāka niṉṟu otturippavāyiṉa veṉpatūum, tīppiḻampāṉatu `ñāyiṟu `tiṅkaḷ `tī eṉa muttiṟappaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ am mūṉṟiṉum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭum vēḷviyiṉiyum muttiṟappaṭumāṟu vittu vēṭṭu vantavar imayam mutaṟ kumariyīṟākak kiṭanta ipparata nāṭṭiṉkaṭ paṇṭuvikiya tamiḻantaṇarē yāvareṉpatūum, vicuvāmittirar mutalāṉa tamiḻantaṇar vēṭṭa muttī vēḷvi civapirāṉmēṟṟāyk kuṭi koli illā aruḷoḻukka vaḻipāṭṭiṉpālatātal paṟṟiyē ammeymmiyiṉi ōtātuṇarnta aruḷāciriyarāṉa tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ ataṉi vētavēḷvi eṉa mīkkūṟuvārāyiṉareṉpatūum, tamiḻantaṇar vēṭṭamuttī vēḷviyiṉ nuṇporuḷ maṟinuṭpam aṟiyappeṟāta parupporuḷaṟiviṉarāṉa āriyap pārppaṉar tāmum avarpōl vēḷviyāṟṟap pukuntu tamakkuriya ūṉiyum kaḷḷiyum paṭittu intiraṉ mutalāṉa ciṟuteyvaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅki veṟiyāṭ ṭayarntu vantami kaṇṭē civacamaya āciriyarāṉa tirunāvukkaracu nāyaṉār avariyum avar ceytupōnta vaḻipāṭukaḷiyum varunti ikaḻnturippā rāyiṉareṉpatūum tamiḻccāṉṟōr vēṭṭa muttī vaḻipāṭṭiṉiyē paṇṭit tamiḻp pērāciriyarāṉa tolkāppiyaṉār, koṭinili kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṉum poruḷatikārac cūttirattiṟ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttiṉ pāṟpaṭuppārāyiṉa reṉpatūum, muttīyum iṟivaṉukkut tirumēṉipōl viḷaṅkuvatōṭu avaṟku mūṉṟuviḻikaḷ pōlavuṅ kāṇappaṭutal kuṟittē iṟivaṉ avi tammik kaṇkaḷāka uṭiya `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉap peyar peṟuvāṉāyiṉa ṉeṉpatūum iṉitu peṟakkiṭantami kaṇṭukoḷka. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟitta muttī vaḻipāṭu, kuṭi kolimutalāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ uṭiyatallāmi paṟṟiyē ataṉi vaṭu nīṅku ciṟappu vāyntateṉa avar ōtiṉār. avar ataṉi avvā ṟōtavē, ēṉi āriyar ceytupōnta vēḷvika ḷellāṅ kuṭi koli mutalāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ uṭiyavātalun tāṉē peṟappaṭum. atuniṟka.
iṉip, paṇṭik kālattut taṇṭamiḻ antaṇar koṇṭa tava vaṭivam muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷuruvat tōṭu otta iyalpiṟṟātal kāṭṭappukka muṟiyil, iṟivaṟku oḷiyuṭip poruḷkaḷ mūṉṟum mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷāka vittu uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭa uṇmi ittuṇi virintamiyiṉ eñciyavaṟṟic curukkamāka eṭuttu ārāyntu kāṭṭutum.
iṟivaṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉkaṇ muṉittu viḷaṅku vōṉākaliṉ, aññāyiṟṟiṉi avaṟkoru tirumukamākak koṇṭu, kīḻpāl eḻūum ñāyiṟṟiṉ ucciyilum mēlpāl viḻūum ñāyiṟṟiṉ ucciyilum vāṉiṉkaṭ paṭarntu kāṇappaṭum mukiṟkuḻāṅka ḷellāṅ katiravaṉṟaṉ cevvoḷi tōyappeṟṟu avaṟkuc cekkarc caṭipōṟ ṟōṉṟutal paṟṟi, avi tāmē avaṟkuc ceñcaṭik kaṟṟikaḷāka vittu nuvalappaṭṭaṉa itaṉāṉaṉṟō iṟivaṟkuc `caṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyaruntoṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarā niṟkiṉṟatu.
iṉi, vāṉiṉkaṭ paṭarntuḷḷa mukiṟkuḻāṅkaḷ attuṇiyum nīrāvip paṭalaṅkaḷē yākiyālum, appaṭalaṅkaḷ tammēṟ kuḷirṅ kāṟṟu vīciyaḷavāṉē vāṉiṉiṉṟum maḻit tuḷikaḷāyk kīḻ iṟaṅkukiyālum, am mukiṟkuḻāṅkaḷi iṟivaṟkuc caṭikkaṟṟikaḷākak kūṟiya tamiḻccāṉṟōr, am mukiṟ kuḻāttiluḷḷa tūyanīri ac caṭikkaṟṟiyil vikiya kaṅki nīrākavum, vāṉiṉiṉṟum malimēl iṟaṅki nilattiliḻiyum am maḻinīri iṟivaṉṟaṉ caṭimuṭiyiṉiṉṟum paṉimali (imayamali)yil vīḻntu kīḻ iḻiyuṅ kaṅkiyāṟākavum vittu uruvakappaṭutti yurippārāyiṉar.
iṉi orunāṭ kāliyil kīḻpāl eḻūum ñāyiṟu putiya iḷamic cevvi vāyntatāy iruttalāṉum, kīḻkaṭaliṉ ellippuṟattiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu ñāyiṟu mēl ivaruṅkāl ak kaṭalnīriṉ mēṟparappellām mayiliṉ niṟampōl nīlamum pacumiyuṅ kālattu oḷirutalāṉum, av viṭiyaṟkāli ñāyiṟṟiṉkaṇ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum iṟivaṟku `iḷiyōṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum murukaṉ eṉṉum peyari vittu, añ ñāyiṟṟiṉ kīḻp pacceṉṟu kāṇappaṭuṅ kaṭaṟparappiṉi avaṉ ūrntu cellum mayilāka vaḻaṅkuvārāyiṉar. paṇṭittamiḻ makkaḷākiya `paratar eṉpār kaṭaṟkariyaṭutta iṭaṅkaḷil mutaṉmutal viki uyir vāḻntaṉar eṉpati mēlē kāṭṭiṉam. avvā ṟuyirvāḻnta mikappaḻiya kālattētāṉ avar tām vaṇaṅkiya muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷi murukaṉ eṉṉum peyarāṉ vaḻaṅkiṉar. kāliñāyiṟṟiṉ kaṭ kaṭpulaṉāy viḷaṅkiya mutalvaṉi iḷiyaṉeṉak koṇṭu vaṇaṅkiya avar, ataṉ piṟpoḻutu mutirnta māli ñāyiṟṟiṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟiya iṟivaṉi mutiyōṉ eṉakkoṇṭu,aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷiṉum avviruvarum oruvarēyātal terintu, avar tam oṟṟumi terippāṉ vēṇṭi am mutiyōṉ `murukaṉ tātiyē yāvaṉ eṉavum mikavuñ civanta niṟattatāy viḷaṅkum ammāli ñāyiṟṟiṉkaṇ vikuvāṉāṉa apperumāṉuñ civaṉ eṉuñ ciṟanta niṟattiṉaṉē yāvaṉeṉavuṅ kaṭippiṭippā rāyiṉar. iv vuṇmi aṟivuṟuttaṟ poruṭṭē civaṉ eṉum nāman taṉakkēyuṭiya cemmēṉi emmāṉ eṉṉum tirunāvukkaracu nāyaṉār aruṇmoḻiyum eḻalāyiṟṟu. atu niṟka.
iṉi, māliñāyiṟṟiṉ mēlpā lelliyellām mikac civantu cuṭaruñ cekkar vāṉattil maṭṭumē mūṉṟāmpiṟi iyaṟkiyāyt tōṉṟutaliṉ, ataṉiyē uruvaka vakiyāṟ civapirāṉṟaṉ ceñcaṭikkaṇ vikum mūṉṟām piṟiyāka vittut tamiḻccāṉṟōr urippārāyiṉa reṉpatu.
iṉit, tīyāṉatu tāṉ paṟṟiya poruḷi muḻutum erittuc cāmparākkutaṟkumuṉ, apporuḷiṉ mēṟcuṟṟu muḻumiyum erittuc cāmparākki ac cāmpar mēlē pūttiruppa uḷḷē tāṉ centaṇalāy muṟuki viḷaṅkum iruvaki iyalpē, iṟivaṉṟaṉ tirumēṉi micippūttu oḷirun tūya veṇṇīṟākavum ataṉuṭ civantu miḷirum at tirumēṉiyākavum vittu uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. iv ariya uruvakattiṉ vaṉappu
tuṭikoḷnēr iṭiyāḷ curicūḻal maṭanti
	tuṇimulik kaṇkaḷtōy cuvaṭu
poṭikoḷvāṉ taḻaliṟ puḷḷipōl iraṇṭu
	poṅkoḷi taṅkumār piṉaṉē
		(tiruvācakam, aruṭpattu, 50)
eṉṟu māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ icitta ceyyuḷiṟ kaṉintu niṟṟal kāṇka.
iṉi, eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷi yellām iṟukappaṟṟi varutti varum āṇavam, māyi, viṉi eṉṉum mummalaṅ kaḷiyum kuttik kalli nīkkavalla pērāṟṟal iṟivaṉ oruvaṉiṭattē maṭṭum uḷatākaliṉ, am mummulaṅkaḷiyum akattē muttiṟamāy niṉṟu tolikkum appērāṟṟalē, puṟattē kāṇappaṭuṅ kaḻukkaṭi allatu muttalivēl (cūlam) āka vittu uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ivvuṇmi,
kōṟṟēṉ moḻikkiḷḷāy, kōtil peruntuṟikkōṉ
māṟṟāri vellum paṭipakarāy -ēṟṟār
aḻukkaṭiyā neñcuruka mummalaṅkaḷ pāyuṅ
kaḻukkaṭikkāṇ kikkoḷ paṭi	(tiruttacāṅkam, 7)
eṉṉun tiruvācakan tiruppāṭṭiṉāl uṇarttappaṭṭami kāṇka.
iṉi, ellāt tuṉpaṅkaḷum piṟattaṟkiṭamāṉa āṇavamalattiṉ kuṟumpukku aṭiyāḷamāvatu puliyē yākaliṉ, ammalattiṉ ceyali oṭukkiniṟkum iṟivaṉatu  niliyē, pulittōli yurittu uṭuttirukkum niliyāka vittu uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉamē iṟivaṉ ṟirumēṉimici yaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷāka moḻiyap paṭuvaṉa vellām arumperu nuṇporuḷkaḷi uḷḷaṭakkiya uruvaka vamippukkaḷē yākum; avi yellām īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ itu mika viriyum. itukāṟuṅ kāṭṭiyaḷavē īṇṭikkup pōtumākaliṉ, ivaṟṟi ittuṇiyiṉ niṟutti mēṟcelvām.
iṟivaṉ ṟiruvuruva aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷāka vittu mēlē kāṭṭiyavāṟu uruvakañceytu, ellāmvalla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi ōr uruvattirumēṉiyiṟ koṇṭu vaḻipaṭum muṟi, ettaṉiyō āyira māṇṭukaḷukku muṟṟoṭṭē nikaḻntu varuvatoṉṟām. iṟivaṉic civanta niṟaṉuṭiya `cēyōṉ ākavum, nīlaniṟaṉuṭiya `māyōṉ ākavuṅkoṇṭu vaḻipaṭun tiruvuruva vaṇakkam.
māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟi yulakamum
cēyōṉ mēya mivari yulakamum
eṉṉun tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārac cūttirattilēyē eṭuttuc collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. `paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭumuṉ av yāṟu pāynta perunilap parappi aracāṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ palyākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉpāṉ civapirāṉ tirukkōyili valamvantu paṇiyum iyalpiṉaṉeṉpatu,
paṇiyiya rattiniṉ kuṭiyē muṉivar
mukkaṭcelvar nakarvalañ ceyaṟkē	 (puṟanāṉūṟu, 6)
eṉṟu avaṉuṭaṉirunta kārikiḻār eṉṉum nallicip pulavar pāṭiya pāṭṭāṉ naṉkupulaṉākiṉṟatu. ippāṇṭi maṉṉaṉ aviyilirunta maṟṟoru nallicip pulavarāṉa neṭṭimiyār eṉpār ivaṉi vāḻttukiṉṟuḻi,
munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē	(puṟanāṉūṟu, 9)
eṉpataṉāṟ paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟu maṇaliṉum pala āṇṭu vāḻkaveṉa avaṉi vāḻttutal koṇṭu, mutukuṭumi eṉum ap pāṇṭivēntaṉum, avaṉatu avikkaḷattup pulavarkaḷāṉa `kārikiḻār, `neṭṭimiyār mutaliyōrum paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṭalkoḷḷappaṭum muṉ aḵtāvatu iṟṟikku aiyāyim āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē iruntōrātal naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum.
iṉi, tolkāppiyam ākkappaṭṭa kālattil muttī vaḻipāṭum, am muttīkkaṇṇum muṉittu viḷaṅkum iṟivaṉ civanta mēṉiyaṉāyc `cēyōṉ eṉa niṟpaṉeṉṉuṅ kōṭpāṭum mikku vaḻaṅkiṉavāyiṉum, `civaṉeṉṉum peyarātalāl, `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṉu peyarālātal iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkum vaṇakkam aññāṉṟu uḷatāyiṟṟilli. `mukkaṇṇaṉ `civaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyattil ōriṭattāyiṉuṅ kāṇappaṭavilli. iṟṟikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kārikiḻāratu ceyyuḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṉuñ col tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭāmiyi uṟṟu nōkkuṅkāl, tolkāppiyaṅ kārikiḻāratu kālattiṟkum paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭu iyaṟṟappaṭṭateṉpatu teḷiyap paṭum. paṭavē, tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil vaṇaṅkappaṭṭa teyvaṅkaḷ murukakkaṭavuḷum, akkaṭavuḷ muṉittu viḷaṅkum ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ tī eṉṉum muttīyum, māyōṉumēyām.
iṉi, irukkuvētattil uruttirak kaṭavuṇmēṟpāṭap paṭṭa pākkaḷiyum, ēṉic ciṟuteyvaṅkaṇmēṟ pāṭappaṭṭa pākkaḷiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkuṅkāl, ēṉiyōrip pāṭiya pākkaḷiṉum, uruttiraṉmēṟ pāṭiyavi mikac cilavātal ārāyccivallārk kellām eḷitiṉ viḷaṅkuvatēyām. ac cilavaṟṟuḷḷum `uruttiraṉ eṉṉum peyaraṉṟi, `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉum peyar kāṇap paṭukiṉṟilatu. irukkuvētap pattu maṇṭilaṅkaḷuḷ, ēṉi oṉpatu maṇṭilaṅkaḷiviṭak kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭatākiya pattām maṇṭilan toṇṇūṟṟiraṇṭām patikam oṉpatāñ ceyyuḷilmaṭṭum ēpi:civa eṉac `civaṉ eṉumpeyar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cukkila ecur vētattiṉ kaṇṇatāṉa `catarutriyattil uruttiraṉ, civaṉ, carvaṉ, pavaṉ, caṅkaraṉ, pacupati, īcāṉaṉ mutalāṉa civapirāṉ peyarkaḷellām oruṅkē kāṇappaṭiṉum, `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarmaṭṭum āṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟilatu. `triyampakaṉ eṉum orucoṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum, aḵtu `ampikiyākiya ammiyōṭu kūṭiṉavaṉ eṉap poruḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa tallāmal, mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉap poruḷ ceyyappaṭu kiṉṟilatu. irukku, ecur mutalāṉa vētaṅkaḷ eṅkaṇum `āyiraṅkaṇkaḷuṭiyavaṉ civapirāṉ eṉpatu collap paṭukiṉṟatēyallāmal `mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉpatu ōriṭattāyiṉuñ collappaṭavilli. paṇṭit teṉṟamiḻ makkaḷiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷip peritun taḻuvi eḻutiya itikācaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷ, upaniṭataṅkaḷ mutalāyiṉ īṇṭikku mēṟkōḷkaḷ ākā. mikap paḻiya tamiḻnūl vaṭanūlkaḷi naṉkāyntu kāṇuṅkāl, ellāmvalla iṟivaṟku, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṉum mūṉṟu oḷikaḷiyum mukkaṇkaḷāka amittuc, civantoḷirum ulakuruviṉiyē avaṟku oru tirumēṉiyākak kaṟpittu avaṉi vaṇaṅkum vaṇakkam paṇṭit tamiḻmakkaṭku maṭṭumē urittātal iṉitu viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
iṉṉum, iraṇṭāyirattu munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻnūlkaḷ tamiḻppāṭṭukkaḷilātal vaṭanūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻip pāṭṭukkaḷilātal, kaṭavuḷi nāṉmukaṉ tirumāl civapirāṉ eṉa muttiṟappaṭuttu `mummūrtti eṉṟōti, ammūvaruṭ civapirāṉum oruvaṉ eṉṉum poruntāk kōṭpāṭu oru ciṟitāyiṉuṅ kāṇap paṭāmiyum, avaṟṟiṉkaṇṇellāñ civapirāṉ piṟappu iṟappu illā ellām valla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākavē vittu moḻiyappaṭutalum peritum niṉiviṟ patikkaṟ pālaṉavākum.
iṉi, uruttirapperumāṉ civanta niṟattiṉaṉāyt tikaḻtalōṭu, ceṉṉiyiṟ cekkarccaṭikaḷum uṭiyaṉā yirukkiṉṟaṉa ṉeṉpatu irukkuvētattiṟ palakālum nuvalappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu. itaṟku, ataṉ mutaṉ maṇṭilattil imā :rutrāya tavasē kapartinē kṣayatvīrāyeṉṟu toṭaṅkum 114 ām patikamē cāṉṟām. itaṉkaṭ pōnta `kapartin eṉṉuñcoṟ `caṭiyaṉ eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ituvum, itaṉōṭotta irukkuvētap patikaṅkaḷ ēṉiyavum nālāyirattu nāṉūṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavākum.
iṉṉuṅ, kiṟittu camayattavarkkuriya mutalākamattil `iṟivaṉ āpirakāmukku etirē tōṉṟi aruḷ ceytakkāl taṉ peyar elcaṭi eṉa moḻintāṉ eṉpatu collap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. mēlum, `el eṉṉum aṭimoḻiyiṉṟic `caṭi eṉṉum peyarē iṟivaṟ kuriya iyaṟpeyarāka `jāp eṉṉum ākamattil muppattorumuṟi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.27 ic `caṭi eṉṉuñcol tamiḻccollātal aṟiyāta īpurumoḻip pulavar, itaṉ piṟappum poruḷun teriyāmiyiṟ peritum iṭarppaṭṭu mayaṅkuvārāyiṉar. `āpirakām mutalāṉa paṇṭināyaṉmārkaḷ tamiḻarē yeṉpatu ārāycciyiṟ pulaṉātaliṉ, avar tamakkut tōṉṟiya iṟivaṉ taṉ peyaric `caṭiyaṉ eṉat tamiḻmoḻiyil aruḷic ceytatu cālap poruntuvatēyām eṉka.
iṉi, iṟṟikku iraṇṭāyirattu munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ `cevvēḷ mēṟpāṭappaṭṭa `kuṉṟam pūtaṉāratu paripāṭaṟ ceyyuḷiṟ, civapirāṉ taṉ caṭik kaṟṟiyiṟ kaṅki nīrit tāṅkiniṟkum iyalpu,
erimalart tāmari iṟivīḻtta peruvāri
viricaṭip poṟiyūḻttu viḻunikar malarēyppat
taṇivuṟat tāṅkiya taṉinilic calatāri
maṇimiṭaṟ ṟaṇṇaṟku matiyāraṟ piṟantōy nī
eṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka. `tirumāliṉ tiruvaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅkiya kaṅki nīric civapirāṉ taṉatu caṭikkaṇ ēṟṟāṉ eṉap piṟkālattu viṇavar muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi iḻitturippāṉ vēṇṭik kaṭṭiviṭṭa poykkati paḻiya tamiḻnūl vaṭanūlkaḷiṟ ciṟituṅ kāṇappaṭāmiyum īṇṭuniṉiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu.
iṉi, iṟivaṉ tirumuṭimēṟ piṟiyamarntu viḷaṅkun tōṟṟam, iṟṟikku āyiratteṇṇūṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ pāṭappaṭṭaṉavākiya.

tāvil tāḷniḻaṟ ṟavirntaṉṟāl ulakē	 (akam, 1)
eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup paḻantamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ ṟeḷitturikkappaṭ ṭiruttal kāṇka. ēṉit tiruvuruva vaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷum iv vakanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuṭkaṇ ōviyatteḻutik kāṭṭiṉāṟpōl attuṇi eḻilpeṟak kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum viyakkaṟpālatoṉṟām.
ivvāṟāka oḷiyoṭukūṭi viḷaṅkum ulakat tōṟṟattiṉiyē uruvaka vakiyāl iṟivaṉṟaṉ tiruvuruvamāka vittu vaḻipaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻc cāṉ ṟōril, antaṇar eṉpār vēṟu muyaṟciyiṟ pukutaliṉṟi, iṟivaṟku vaḻipāṭu āṟṟutaliṉum, iṟivaṉ nūl ōtutaliṉumē karuttu oruppaṭṭu niṉṟaṉarākaliṉ, avarellām av viṟivaṉ ṟiruvuruvattōṭu otta tavavaṭivamē tamakkum uriyatākak koṇṭiruppārāyiṉar aracaric cārntār perumpāṉmiyum av varacarō ṭotta kōlattiṉiyum, muṉivaric cārntār perum pāṉmiyum am muṉivarōṭotta kōlattiṉiyum, ēṉip palvaki niliyiṉ niṟpāric cārntār perum pāṉmiyum avvan niliyiṉarkkuriya kōlattiṉiyun tān tām mēṟkoḷḷutal iyalpātalpōla, ellām valla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷic cārntārum perumpāṉmiyum ak kaṭavuṭkuriya tirukkōlattiṉit tāmum mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukutalum iyalpāyiṟṟeṉpatu. eṉavē, iṟivaṉ poṉ tōṉṟa oḷivaṭiviṉaṉātal paṟṟi avaṉ vaḻiyil niṟpārāṉa aruntavattōr tāmun taḻalvaṭiviṉatākiya kāviyāṭi uṭuppārāyiṉar. taḻaltāṉ paṟṟiya poruḷit tūya cāmparākki ac cāmparit taṉmēṟ pūkka aṇintirukkum iyalpē iṟivaṉṟaṉ tirumēṉimicit timirntirukkun tirunīṟṟiṉ uṇmiyātal kaṇṭu, avaṉi vaḻipaṭuñ cāṉṟōrun tantirumēṉi mēlun tirunīṟu pūcāniṟpar. iṟivaṉ taṉatu neṟṟimēṟ kaṇṇoṭu mukkaṇṇaṉāy viḷaṅkutalōṭu oppavē, cāṉṟōrun tamatu neṟṟimicic cāntiṉāṟ poṭṭiṭṭiruppar. iṟivaṉ taṉ tirumuṭimēṟ caṭikkaṟṟi uṭiyaṉāy iruttal pōlavē, aruntamiḻt tāpatarun tañceṉṉimēṟ caṭimuṭiyuṭiya rāyiruppar. iṟivaṉ taṉ caṭikkaṟṟimicic cumantirukkuṅ kaṅkinīrkku aṭiyāḷamākavē, tamiḻt tāpatarun tam kiyil taṇṇīrk kuṭuvi ēntiyiruppar. iṟivaṉippōl taṇṇīrit tām talimicit tāṅkutal ākāmiyiṉ, aruntavattōr ataṉit taliyilaṉṟik kiyiṉkaṭ ṭāṅkuvārāyiṉa reṉṟu uṇarntukoḷka. iṉṉum, mummalaṅkaḷic cekukkum iṟivaṉatu pērāṟṟalē avaṉ taṉ kiyil ēntiniṟkum muttali vēlākac collappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉatu tiruk kōlattit tāṅkiya tāpatarum `mukkōl ēntiyiruppar; itupaṟṟiyē ivar `mukkōṟ pakavareṉavum moḻiyappaṭuvar28. iṉṉum, āṇavamalattiṉ valiyi yoṭukkiya iṟivaṉatu niliyē, avaṉ pulittōli uṭuttiyirukkum iruppātaliṉ, avaṉvaḻic cārnta tāpatar tāmum pulittōli mēṟkoṇṭiruppar; iṉi, `māṉ eṉṉuñcol tamiḻil māṉ eṉṉum vilaṅkiṉiyum, vaṭamoḻiyiṟ `pirakirutimāyiyiṉiyuṅ kuṟippatoṉ ṟākaliṉ29 māyiyi yoṭukkiniṟkum nilikku aṭiyāḷa māka māṉṟōli irukkiyāyk koṇṭum iruppa reṉka. iṉṉum ellā nūṟkalvikkum iṟivaṉē talivaṉ eṉpataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka avaṉ taṉatu valakkiyiṟ cuvaṭiyoṉṟēnti amarntiruttal pōla, avaṉ vaḻippaṭṭa tāpatarun tamatu kiyil nūl oṉṟēntiyiruppa reṉka. ivvāṟāka iṟivaṉōṭotta vaṭivukoḷḷun tamiḻantaṇariṉ tavavaṭiva aṭiyāḷaṅ kaḷiṟ cila, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉarāl,
nūlē karakam mukkōli maṇiyē
āyuṅ kāli antaṇarkku uriya
eṉṟu ōtappaṭṭami kāṇka. ic cūttirattiṉkaṭ collappaṭṭa `nūl eṉpatu `kalinūlē yallāmaṟ pūṇul aṉṟu. tolkāppiyaṉār nūl eḻutiya paṇṭikkālattiṟ purinūl pūṇum vaḻakkam uṇṭeṉpati nāṭṭutaṟkuc cāṉṟu iṉmiyāṉum, tuṟavoḻukkattiṉ niṟpārkkup pūṇūl iṉmi vaṭanūlārkkum uṭampāṭakalāṉum,30 itaṟkup pūṇul eṉṟu urikūṟiya piṟkālat turikārarāṉa `pērāciriyar kūṟṟup poruntāmi kaṇṭukoḷka. `karakam eṉpatu nīrkkuṭuvi. `mukkōl iṟivaṉēntiya muttali vēlukku aṭiyāḷam. `maṇi iruttaṟku iṭum pulittōl māṉṟōl mutaliyaṉa. iṉṉun tavavoḻukkam mēṟkoṇṭa antaṇar talimicic caṭimuṭiyuṭiyarā yiruppareṉpatu,
kaṭunteṟaṟ centī vēṭṭup
puṟantāḻ puricaṭi pularttu vōṉē	(251)
eṉṉum paḻiya puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷāl iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum. iṉṉum avar kāviyāṭi pūṇṭu mukkōl kikkoṇṭu tavavoḻukkattiṉ niṟṟal,
kaltōyttu uṭutta paṭivap pārppāṉ,
mukkōl acinili kaṭuppa
eṉṉum paḻiya mullippāṭṭu aṭikaḷāṟ pulaṉā kiṉṟatu. iṉṉum avar mēṟkūṟiya tava aṭiyāḷaṅ kaḷōṭu, tan tirumēṉimēl tirunīṟum pūciyiruppareṉpatu,
cuttiya pokkaṇattu eṉpaṇi
kaṭṭaṅkañ cūḻcaṭi veṇ
pottiya kōlattiṉīr	(242)
eṉṟu tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyāril māviratiyar viḷikkappaṭumāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉamākap paṇṭikkālattut taṇṭamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta tāpatarellārum,ellām valla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi `murukaṉ
 `civaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl muttīkkaṇ vaṇaṅki, am muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷukkut tām uruvaka vakiyāṟ kaṟpitta vaṭivattiyē tāmum pūṇṭu, tavavoḻukkattiṉ kaṇṇarāy āṅkāṅku vikiṉamiyē paḻiya centamiḻ nūlkaḷāṉ aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟoḻukiya aruntavattōriṉ vaṭivamum, avar tan tavavoḻukkamum, av voḻukkattiṉ pālatāṉa nōṉpum, pārppaṉariṉ tuṟavoḻukkamumē `paṭimi `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷāl uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati mēlē 654, 656 ām pakkaṅkaḷiṟ paḻiyanūl mēṟkōḷkaḷ pala koṇṭu naṉku viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉēm. tolkāppiyam mutalāka vanta paṇṭit tamiḻnūlkaḷil ōriṭattāṉum `paṭimi `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, camaṇmuṉivariṉ tavavoḻukkattiṉi uṇarttutaṟku mēṟkōḷ kaṇṭilam. karikāṟcōḻaṉ kālattiṟku aḵtāvatu iṟṟikku āyirattut toḷḷāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉkaṭ camaṇcamayamātal ataṉit taḻuviṉōrātalirunta reṉpataṟku ētoru cāṉṟum paḻiya tamiḻnūlkaḷ tamiḻppāṭṭukaḷil ōr eṭṭuṇiyuṅ kāṇappaṭavilli.
uṇmi yivvāṟiruppat, tolkāppiyaṉāric camaṇ muṉivareṉa nāṭṭappukkavar avar kālattiṟ camaṇmatan tamiḻnāṭṭil iruntateṉṉun tamatu kūṟṟi meyccāṉṟu oṉṟutāṉuṅ kāṭṭi niṟuvāmal avaric camaṇareṉak kūṟappukkatu, paḻantamiḻ nūlārāycci yuṭiyārāl nakiyāṭi eḷḷappaṭutaṟkē iṭaṉāyatu kāṇka. mēlum avar `paṭimi eṉṉuñ collukkut `tavavoḻukkam eṉṉum poruḷ uḷatātalik kaṇṭilēm eṉavum, tām viṉaviya vaṭamoḻip pulavar kaḷuṅkūṭa accollum poruḷum illiyeṉṟu teḷintēm eṉavum urippārāyiṉar.31
paḻiya tamiḻnūlkaḷi ārāyntupārāmalē avar aṅṅaṉan tuṇivuri nikaḻttiyatu iraṅkaṟpālatoṉṟām.
kūṟiṉi perumaniṉ paṭimi yāṉē
eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattilum,
taṇṭoṭu piṭitta tāḻkamaṇ ṭalattup
  paṭiva vuṇṭip pārppaṉa makaṉē
eṉṉuṅ kuṟuntokiyilum,
kaltōyt tuṭutta paṭivap pārppāṉ
eṉṉum mullip pāṭṭilum `paṭimi `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷantaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkatti uṇarttutal muṉṉarē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉām.ac coṟkaḷ aṅṅaṉam ap poruḷ uṇarttum vaḻiyellāñ civacamayat tuṟavi kaḷiyē uṇarttak kāṇṭumaṉṟic, camaṇ tuṟavikaḷi oru ciṟitāyiṉum uṇarttak kāṇkilēm.
mēluñ, camaṇ camayattiṉar muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṉum nampikkiyuṭiyarallar; annampikki yillāmiyiṉ, muttīvēṭṭu vaḻipaṭum nīrarum. allar; aḵtillāmiyiṉ, tī niṟattatākiya kāviyāṭiyum, tīyiṉ eccamākiya tūya veṇṇīṟum mēṟkoḷvārum allar; iṟivaṉiṉ tiruvaṭiyāḷamākiya caṭi muṭiyun; taṇṇīrk kuṭuviyum, mukkōlum uṭiyārum allar; ip peṟṟiyiṉarāṉa camaṇ muṉivar `ācīvakarum, `nikaṇṭarum, `muṇṭacāvakarum eṉa muttiṟappaṭuva reṉpatūum, ivaruḷ ācīvakarāvār āṭiyiṉṟi muṟṟum ammaṇamāyum, nikaṇṭar iṭuppil oruciṟu tuṇṭu maṭṭum uṭuppavarāyum muṇṭa cāvakar ariyil oru tuṇṭu uṭuttut taliyi maḻittuviṭupavarāyum iruppareṉpatūum aṅkut taranikāyattiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ic camaṇmuṉivarum, ivariṉ vēṟāṉa putta muṉivarkaḷum kutama cākkiyar kālattil iruntāṟpōlavē, taliyiṟ caṭimuṭiyuṭiyaruṅ kiyil mukkōl ēntiyavarumāṉa pārppaṉat tuṟavikaḷum akkālattil vaṭanāṭṭilum iruntaṉareṉpatūum av aṅkuttara nikāyattilēyē nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.32 eṉavē, kutamacākkiyar irunta kālattilēyē aḵtāvatu iṟṟikku iraṇṭāyirattin nūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarēyē vaṭanāṭṭiluñ civavaṭivam pūṇṭa pārppaṉa muṉivarkaḷ iruntami putta camaya nūlkaḷiṉālēyē pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō? maṟṟut teṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭilō akkālattiṟ pārppaṉa muṉivarit tavira, ēṉic camaṇ putta muṉivar iruntilar. ākavē, `paṭimi eṉṉuñ coṟṟeṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta civa antaṇariṉ tava oḻukkattiyun tavavaṭivattiyuñ cuṭṭutaṟkē eḻuntataṉṟic, camaṇ muṉivariṉ tavavoḻukkatti uṇarttutaṟ keḻuntataṉ ṟeṉpatu kaṭippiṭikka atuvēyumaṉṟi, vaṭanāṭṭilirunta camaṇ muṉivariṉ tavavoḻukkatti uṇarttuñ collāka atu camaṇ camaya nūlkaḷilāyiṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatōveṉa yām an nūlkaḷi ārāyntu pārkka, āṇṭum `paṭimā eṉṉuñ collik kaṇṭilēm. ac col camaṇ muṉivariṉ tava voḻukkattiyē kuṟikku meṉa nāṭṭappuk kavarātal, ataṟku oru mēṟkōḷāyiṉuñ camaṇ nūlkaḷilirun teṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉarō veṉṟāl, atutāṉum illi. `paṭimā eṉpatu camaṇ camayac collē eṉpataṟku ētoru mēṟkōḷum paḻiya camaṇ nūlkaḷilirun teṭuttuk kāṭṭātu, ippōtu cila āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ōr āṅkila mātar eḻutiya ōr āṅkila nūli nampi accol camaṇ camayattavarkkē yurittātal vēṇṭumeṉat tan tuṇipurittaliṉumpiḻipāṭāvatu piṟitilli; ip piḻipāṭṭuriyik koṇṭu āciriyar tolkāppiya ṉāric camaṇa muṉivareṉa kāṭṭap pukuvatiṉum pētimiyāvatum piṟitilli. ārāycci muṟi iṉṉateṉṟaṟiyātār tāmum iṉṉōraṉṉa ārāycciyiṟ pukutal peritum ētamāmeṉka.
ippōtu vaḻaṅkuñ camaṇ nūlkaḷil vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyiliruppaṉavellām ki.pi.āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattiliruntu iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa vākumeṉa avi tammi naṉkārāynta airōppiya āciriyarkaḷ kūṟāniṟkiṉṟaṉar.33 ivvāṟu piṟpaṭṭa kālattil eḻutappaṭṭa camaṇnūlkaḷil yāṇṭō arukip `paṭimi eṉṉuñ col camaṇ muṉivariṉ tavavoḻukkatti yuṇarttutaṟku vaḻaṅkappaṭṭirukkalām. atu koṇṭu accol camaṇ camayattavarkkē uritteṉṟal ārāycciyuṇarvillār kūṟṟām. ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇ ṭilirun  teḻutappaṭṭa camaṇ nūlkaṭku ainnūṟāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭa aruntamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉum pāṭṭukaḷiṉu mellām accol antaṇariṉ civa tavavoḻukkattiyē uṇarttak kāṇṭalāl, atu civa camayattavarkkē urittāvataṉṟic camaṇmatattavarkku urittātal oruvāṟṟāṉuñ cellātu. tamakku muṉṉirunta civa antaṇat tāpatartan tavavoḻukkattukku vaḻaṅki vanta ap `paṭimi yeṉṉuñ colliyē piṉvanta camaṇ muṉivar tamakkum uriyatāka orōviṭattup payaṉpaṭuttiṉā rākaṟpālār. vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kutama cākkiyar kālattirunta putta camaṇ camayattavaruṅ kūṭat, taṅ kālattiṟku muṉṉirunta civa antaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkat tiyē mutalāka vittu, ataṉpaṭi tamakkuriya oḻukalāṟukaḷi variyaṟuttamippā rāyiṉareṉa, ivi tammip palapaṭiyāṉum naṭuniṉṟu naṉkāynta hermaṉ jākōpi eṉṉum airōppiya āciriyar naṉku viḷakkikkāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟār.34 vaṭanāṭṭiṟ paṇṭik kālattirunta civa antaṇat tāpatar civapirāṉ tiruvuruva aṭiyāḷamāṉa kāviyāṭiyē uṭuttut talimēṟ caṭimuṭiyuṭiyarā yiruntaṉa reṉpatu, avar tamatu `jiṉacūttira āṅkila moḻipeyarppiṉ mukavuriyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya pōtāyaṉa tarumacāttira mēṟkōḷ kaḷāl (2, 6, 11, 15 - 21) naṉku viḷaṅkum. civa antaṇa muṉivar uṭuttuṅ kāviyāṭiyip pārttē, putta piṭcukkaḷ niṟattāl ataṉi yoppatoru tuvarāṭi yuṭupparāyiṉa reṉpatūum, putta camaṇmuṉivar tuvakkattil vaṭanāṭṭiṉ oru ciṟu pakutiyil iruntamipōlātu civamuṉivarar paratanāṭeṅkum paṇṭutoṭṭē paraviyiruntaṉa reṉpatūum piṟavum avarāl naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. uṇmiyārāycciyiṟ  ṟērnta ivvirōppiya āciriyar eṭuttukkāṭṭiya paṇṭi vaṭanūl mēṟkōḷkaḷiyum, avaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu ārāyntu kāṭṭiya uṇmi muṭipukaḷiyum naṭuniṉṟu nōkkavallārkkup, putta camaṇmuṉivar tōṉṟutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟṟoṭṭē civa antaṇa aruntavattōr ip parata nāṭeṅkum paraviyiruntamiyum, piṉvanta putta camaṇ tuṟavōr tamakkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṉṉirunta civa antaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkattiyē perumpāṉmiyun taḻuvi yoḻukalāṉamiyun teṟṟeṉap pulaṉākā niṟkum. ākavē, tavavoḻukkamum, at tavavoḻukkatti yuṇarttum `paṭimi, `paṭivam `pratimā eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷuñ, civa antaṇariṉiṉṟu ēṉip puttacamaṇ camayattārkku vantatākal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟip, piṟkālat tavarāṉa puttar camaṇariliruntu, ivarkkup pallāyiram āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭa civa muṉivarkkuc ceṉṟatākal ēlātu. ātalāl, muṟkālattatu itu, piṟkālattatu itu eṉṟu pakuttārāyamāṭṭātu, muṉṉatu piṉṉatākavum piṉṉatu muṉṉatākavum piṟaḻakkoṇṭu, paṭimiyoḻukkañ camaṇarkkē yuriyateṉak karintāratu uri periyatoru talitaṭumāṟṟa vuriyāy muṭintami kaṇṭu koḷka.
aṟṟēlaḵtāka, tavavoḻukkamum ataṟkēṟṟa tava vaṭivamum ataṉi yuṇarttum `paṭimi eṉṉuñ collum, puttar camaṇar tōṉṟutaṟkup pallāyira āṇṭukaḷ muṉṉarē civa antaṇarpāl uḷavāyatu uṇmiyāyiṉ, ataṉi niṟuvutaṟkuriya paḻiya nūṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷ kāṭṭuka veṉiṉ; avi tammip paṇṭit tamiḻnūlkaḷiṉiṉṟum muṉṉarē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉām. ap paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ pāṭṭukaḷil yāṇṭum putta camaṇ camayattavar ōreṭṭuṇiyuṅ kuṟikkappaṭāmiyum, `paṭimi, `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ civa antaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkattiyē kuṟittaṟku avaṟṟuḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭutalum āṇṭē viḷakkip pōntām. ākavē, puttar camaṇar tōṉṟutaṟku mūvāyira āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatākiya tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyira vuriyiṟ pōnta `paṭimiyōṉ eṉṉuñcoṟ civa antaṇa muṉivaṉākiya āciriyaṉ tolkāppiyaṉṟaṉ tavavoḻuk kattiyun tavavaṭivattiyum maṭṭumē cuṭṭutal vēṇṭumaṉṟi, avaṟkup peruṅkālam piṟpaṭṭirunta puttar camaṇariṉ tavavoḻukkattiyātal tavavaṭivattiyātal cuṭṭutal oru ciṟitum iciyāteṉ ṟuṇarntukoḷka. tol kāppiyattuḷ yāṇṭum puttacamaṇ camayaṅkaḷ oru tiṉittaṉiyuṅ kuṟippiṭap paṭāmiyum niṉiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu.
iṉṉuṅ, kutamacākkiyarum makāvīrarun tōṉṟi muṟiyē puttacamaṇ camayaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvittaṟku muṉ, cākkiya vakuppiṉar civapirāṉiyē vaṇaṅki vantaṉa reṉpatu, vaṭanāṭṭiluḷḷa paḻiya kalveṭṭukaḷiṉ ārāycciyāl naṉku pulaṉākāniṟkiṉṟatu.35 kutama cākkiyar piṟanta ñāṉṟu, avar civapirāṉ tiruvaruḷip peṟutaṟ poruṭṭuc civapirāṉ tirukkōyiṟkup peṟṟōrāl eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭami, paḻiya puttacamayak kalveṭṭu oṉṟiṟ cetukki vikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.36 mikac ciṟanta ic cāṉṟukaḷi uṟṟārāya vallār evarkkum, putta camaṇmataṅkaḷ tōṉṟutaṟkup pallāyira āṇṭukaḷ muṟṟoṭṭē ip paratanā ṭeṅkaṇum paravi yiruntatu civacamayamēyā meṉpatūum, iṅku muḻutum ulaviya tāpatar civacamaya antaṇa muṉivarēyāvareṉpatūun teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
aṟṟēl, `pratimā, `paṭimi `paṭivam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟēṉum, puttacamaṇ nūlkaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa paḻiya vaṭanūlkaḷuḷ uṇṭō veṉiṉ; uṇṭu. vaṭanūlkaḷuḷ mikap paḻiyaṉa irukku, ecur, cāmam eṉpavikaḷēyām. aiyāyira āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ tamiḻar āriyar eṉṉum iruvakuppiṉarkkum āciriyarāyirunta muṉivarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭuc citarntukiṭanta ivaṟṟiṉ pāṭṭukaḷ pāratap pōr muṭintapiṉ viyācarāl oruṅku tokukkappaṭṭu irukku, ecur, cāmam eṉṉum mūṉṟu vētaṅkaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvētappāṭṭukaḷ vaḻaṅkiya kālattil vaṇaṅkappaṭṭu vanta intiraṉ, varuṇaṉ, uruttiraṉ mutalāṉa teyvaṅkaḷukku uruvaṅkaḷ camittu vittu, avaṟṟiṟku vaḻipāṭu āṟṟivantaṉar eṉpatum avi tammik karuttūṉṟi ārāyumvaḻi iṉitu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. āriyar pōrmēṟ ceṉṟa kālaṅkaḷil intiraṉatu vaṭivatti eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉareṉpatu irukkuvētattiṟ collappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu.37 civapirāṉ tiruvaṭiyāḷamāṉa civaliṅka vaṭivattip paṇṭit tamiḻmakkaḷ vittuvaḻipaṭṭami irukkuvēta ēḻām maṇṭilattiṉ irupattōrām patikattilum pattām maṇṭilattiṉ toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatām patikattilum viḷakkamāka vittu nuvalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. iviyēyaṉṟi, `pratimā eṉṉuñ collē oru teyvampōṟ ceyta vaṭivattik kuṟikkum moḻiyāka ecurvētattil nataya pratimā ati (32, 3) eṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. iv vuṇmikaḷic ciṟitāyiṉum ārāyntu pārkkum aṟivu matukiyiṉṟi, `pratimā eṉṉuñ col camaṇ nūlkaḷi laṉṟip, paḻiya vētanūlkaḷil yāṇṭu milli yeṉṟu tuṇinturittavar tam pētimip perukki eṉṉeṉpēm! iṉiyēṉum accol putta camaṇ nūlkaḷ tōṉṟutaṟku mūvāyira āṇṭu muṉṉirunta ecurvētattilēyē uḷateṉṟaṟintu avar uṇmi teḷiyakkaṭavārāka.
iṉi, avar,  tolkāppiyaric camaṇcamayattiṉareṉa nāṭṭutaṟku maṟṟumoru cāṉṟu kāṭṭuvē meṉap pukuntu, uyirkaḷi ōraṟivumutal aiyaṟivu īṟāka uḷḷa aintu vakikaḷiṟ pakuttaṭakkutal camaṇcamayak kōṭpāṭāku meṉṟun tolkāppiyaṉār ivvivaki uyirp pākupāṭukaḷit tamatu `marapiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷil eṭuttōtutaliṉ avar camaṇ camayattavarēyātal vēṇṭumeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉār. ituvum oru ciṟitu ārāyaṟpāṟṟu.
camaṇ matattavarkaḷ evvakippaṭṭa uyiriyum evvaki ētupaṟṟiyum kollalākātu eṉṉum mikac ciṟanta kollā aṟattik kaṭippiṭittu niṉṟavarkaḷ. vaṭanāṭṭiṉkaṇ vantu kuṭiyēṟiya āriyarum avar vaḻic cārnta pārppaṉarum vēḷvi vēṭṭali ōr ētuvāka vittukkoṇṭu, eṇṇiṟanta vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi neyyiṟ porittu uṇṭuñ, cōmap pūṇṭil iṟakkiya kaḷḷi nirampa aruntiyum veṟiyāṭ ṭayarntu vantamiyāl, iyaṟkiyilēyē pulāluṅ kaḷḷum maṟutta civavēḷāḷa aracar (kṣattiriyar) av vāriyartan tīya oḻukalāṟṟiṉi aruvaruttu, ataṉit taṭiceytaṟ poruṭṭākavē camaṇ puttakak koḷkikaḷit tōṟṟuvittu, avaṟṟi eṅkum paravac ceytaṉar. camaṇmata āciyarāṉa `makāvīrarum puttacamaya āciriyarāṉa `kutama cākkiyarum vaṭakkē makatanāṭṭil vikiya kṣattiriya allatu tamiḻ vēḷāḷa aracamarapiṟ piṟantōrē yāvar. āriyar vaṭanāṭṭiṟ pukuntu āṅkāṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyapōtu, aṅkam, makatam, kāci, kōcalam, vaccī, malli, cētī, vañcam, kuru, pāñcālam, maccam, cūracēṉam, accakam, avanti, kāntāram, kāmpōcam mutaliya patiṉāṟu nāṭukaḷil nākarikattiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya vēṟu marapiṉarāṉa tamiḻ vēḷāḷarē aracupurintaṉar eṉpatu aṅkuttaranikāyattil (1, 213; 4, 252, 256, 260) cevvaṉē kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. makata nāṭṭilirunta tamiḻar,  muṉṉarē yām viḷakkiyeḻutiya pūrukkaḷic cērntōrāvar.38 iṅṅaṉamākac camaṇ kōṭpāṭṭip paravac ceyta makāvīrar tamiḻaraca muṉivarēyākiyāl, avarkkum akkālattirunta ēṉi vēḷāḷarkkum potuvāka uriya kollā aṟattiavar valiyuṟuttip paravac ceytatil ētum putumiyilli. aññāṉṟirunta attamiḻ muṉivar, taṅ kālattiṟkup pallāyira āṇṭu muṉṉirunta āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār mutalāṉa tamiḻc cāṉṟōr kaṇṭuritta uyirp pākupāṭukaḷiyē tāmun taḻuvik kūṟiṉārallatu, makāvīrar mutalākap piṉvanta camaṇmuṉivar vakuttataṉik kaṇṭu tolkāppiyaṉār avi tammik kūṟiṉārallar.
aḵteṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum: evviṭattiluḷḷa uyiriyuṅ kollalākā teṉpatē camaṇartaṅ kōṭ pāṭākiyāl, ak kōṭpāṭṭiṟku iciyavē uyirkaḷ uyirvāḻum iṭaṅkaḷi avarkaḷ mutalil ārāyn turippārāyiṉar. uyirkaḷ nilattiluḷḷaṉavum, nīriluḷḷaṉavum, neruppiluḷ ḷaṉavuṅ, kāṟṟiluḷḷaṉavum eṉa nālvakip paṭumeṉṟu urittu, āṇṭāṇṭuḷḷa uyirkaḷukku nōyuñ cākkāṭum uṇṭākāvaṇṇam, avaṟṟōṭu iyintu kāṇappaṭum uyiril poruḷkaḷit tām payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum muṟikaḷi viḷakkik kāṭṭutalē camaṇcamaya āciriyar karuttākum. ippōtuḷḷa camaṇ nūlkaḷil mikap paḻiyatākiya ācāraṅkacūttiram, curutakantam, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, ēḻām attiyāyaṅkaḷil, uyirkaḷ ulavutaṟkiṭamāṉa nilaṉnīr neruppu kāṟṟu eṉṉum iṭavakiyāl avvuyirkaḷi nālvakippaṭuttu, avi tamakku iṭar nērāvaṇṇam aṅkaṅkuḷḷa ēṉip poruḷkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttumāṟu viritturittal kāṇka; itaṉ āṟām attiyayaṉattil, īraṟivu mūvaṟivu nālaṟivu aiyaṟivu uṭiya ellā uyirkaḷukkum iṉpa tuṉpamum accamum uḷavākaliṉ, avi tamakku enta ētuvik koṇṭum evvaki iṭaruñ ceytalākā teṉṉum attuṇiyē vaṟpuṟutturikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟa tallāmal, ōraṟivu mutal aiyaṟivu īṟāka vittu uyirkaḷip pākupāṭu ceytal āṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭavilli. ākavē, uyirkaḷi iṭavakiyāl nālvakippaṭa vakuttuk kāṭṭutalē paṇṭic camaṇ āciriyartaṅ karuttātal kāṇṭumaṉṟi, aṟivuvakiyāṟ pakuttukkāṭṭutal avarkkuk karuttātal kaṇṭilam. iṭavakiyāṟ pakuttalēavartaṅ kōṭpāṭṭukkup payaṉpaṭuvataṉṟi, aṟivu vakiyāṟ pakuttal ataṟkuc ciṟitum payaṉpaṭuvataṉṟām; eṉṉi? īraṟivuṭiya vāyiṉum allatu aiyaṟivuṭiyavāyiṉum ellāvuyirkkum iṉpatuṉpamum accamum uṭiyavākiyāl, aṟiviṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu karutātu ellāvuyirkaḷiyum pātukāttalē ceyaṟpāla teṉpatūum, uyirkaḷ ōriṭattum uḷa ōriṭattum ila eṉa aṟiyāmiyāṟ karutiviṭātu nilaṉ nīr neruppu kāṟṟu eṉṉum nāṉkiṭaṅkaḷilum avi uḷavātalik karuttūṉṟi yārāyntu pārttal iṉṟiyamiyātateṉpatūum avartaṅ karuttākali ṉeṉka. eṉavē, uyirkaḷi iṭavakiyāl ārāytalē paṇṭic camaṇāciriyar kōṭpāṭākumallatu aṟivu vakiyāl ārāytal avartam kōṭpāṭākāmi peṟṟām. peṟavē, uyirkaḷi iṭavakiyāṟ pakuttu virittuk kāṭṭiya paḻiya ācāraṅka cūttirattiṟku māṟāka, avi tammi iṭavakiyālum aṟivu vakiyālum pakuttu viritturitta uttirāttiyayaṉanūl (34) mikavum piṟpaṭṭa kālatteḻunta camaṇmuṉivarāl tolkāppiyattip pārttuc ceyyappaṭṭa toṉṟāka vēṇṭumākaliṉ, aḵtu īṇṭikku mēṟkōḷākāteṉa maṟukka. ācāraṅka cūttirattiṟkuc cīlāṅka muṉivar eḻutiyavari ki.pi. 876 il muṭikkappaṭṭa toṉṟākiyāl,39 ācāraṅka cūttirattiṉ kālaṅ ki.pi. aintu allatu āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭatākātu. `uttarāttiyayaṉam camaṇ nūlkaḷil mikap piṟpaṭṭatātalum, ataṉ iṟutik kaṇ uyirkaḷi iṭavakiyālum aṟivuvakiyālum pākupāṭu ceytu virittōtum muppattāṟām attiyayaṉam iṉṉum piṟpaṭṭa kālattē ceytu cērkkappaṭṭatātalum mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa airōppiya āciriyarāl naṉku viḷakkik kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa40 paḻiyatākiya `ācāraṅka cūttiram mutaṟkaṇ uyirkaḷi iṭavakiyāṟ pakuttu virittōti aṟivuvakiyāṟ pakuttali oru coṟṟo ṭaraḷavil mikac curukkik kūṟāniṟka, `uttarāttiyayaṉamō iṭavakiyāṉ maṭṭumēyaṉṟi aṟivu vakiyālum uyirkaḷip pakuttu mika virittōtā niṟkiṉṟatu; 20.	It was bot so much an invasion as an advance, and we can easily imagine that it must have an achievement of no small difficulty for a body of men necessarily very inferior in numbers, in the face of compact population, brave, stubborn, and strongly organized. Such the Dravidians are now, when the number, over twentyeight millions south of the Vindhya, and there is not the slightest reason to doubt that such in the min, they were at the early time of their long patriotic struggle Vedic India, p. 295.
21.	It will be surprise to many that the Aryan population of the Indian continent should be so out of all proportion small when compared to the descendants and representatives of those races which the Aryan immigrants found in possion. The same difference must have existed on a still greater scale in those earliest times - and would alonse suffiee to stamp as irrational the theory of Aryan supremacy having been established by sheer conquest and force” Ibid,p.314.
22.	To keep strictly within the information supplied by the Rig-Veda itself - Vasishtha was the bard of the Tritsu the leading and purest Aryan tribe, and Visvamitra was the bard of the Bharatas, their great enemies and one of the most powerful native tribes. He at one time had been with the Tritsu, and for whatever cuse he left them -not improbably personal revenge - he played a conspicuous part in the confederacy which attempted to check the Aryan advance and increasing power. There is a hymn (53), in Book III, that of the Visvamitra family, which evidently alludes to this very thing. In the first part of the hymn it is sid that when visvamitra conducted kings sudas sacrifies, Indra was gracious to him for the Rishi’s sake, and a great blessing is pronounced on the king, and his war -steed and the expedition on which he starts,. Then , quite suddenly, visvamitry is made to declare in his own person, that his prayers protect of imprecation aginst enemies who are not named, but whom tradition so positively inentified with vasishtha and his family that the priests of this house in later times never uttered these four verses, and tried not to hearthem when spoken by other Brahmans. It is most probable that the Visavamitras resented some distinction conferred upon the vasishthas, possibly their appointment as purohitas to the Tritsu royal family and went over to theri most powerful enemies, the Purus andharatas. The Tritsu and their allies were veictorious in the ensuingstruggle known as “the war of the ten kings” and both the bards have lfet descriptions of it and of the final battle on the Parushni, in some spirited hymns, the most undoubtedly historical of the collection. At a later period the followers of  Vasishtha and his descendants represent the narrowl orthodox Brahminic school, with its petty punctillousness in the matter of forms, rites observances, its intolerance in the matter of forms, rites, observances, its on tolerance of everthing in -Aryan, its rigid separatism. This school it was which stood guard through all these ages. and up to our day the champion -and possibly orginally the institutor of case who advanced and upheld all the exaggerated clims of the Brahman priesthoo, to divinity. To the rule of the world and ownership of all it holds, to supernatural compelling powers over nature and the gods themselves through sacrifice and ascetic practices. and the likd. The followers of visvamitra nd his descendants, on the other hand, represented the school of liberalism and progress of conciliation and amalgamation. Vedic Indian, pp. 318 - 320.

The Vishnin bracketed with the Siva, which is thought to be a name of the Tugra, one of the oldest aboriginal Dravidian peoples, whom the Aryas had specially nick named 	 ‘Sons of the Serpent’ and who under the religious designation of Siva, were very probably the originators of the worship of siva under the form or with the attribute of a snake:” Ibid, p.328.
